    - **Scenario audit (same day), fixed:** (a) the weekly send claims its once-a-week key *before* building pictures, so a failure (e.g. Pillow missing) used to skip the whole week -- the claim is now released on error and the next 10-min tick retries; (b) a change Noorish makes Sunday evening that is still held when the 9 PM digest goes out would be announced again an hour later -- the digest now drops held changes that fall inside the week it just showed; (c) turning a timed event into a WFH/office/holiday day sent nothing (the event announcement had already gone out) -- it now counts as a new entry; (d) `_ist_now` falls back to `datetime.now(IST)` so a clock-helper failure can't lose a held alert; (e) `POST /api/companion/week-digest` accepts `?to=all` and `&week=this` (current Mon-Sun instead of the next one), used for the 2026-10-12 manual send to all 9 active people. **Second pass (same day) -- the old "known limits" were fixed:** (1) delete-then-recreate of the same entry nets out (a removal identical to an addition cancels, nothing sent); (2) **hold time is now 30 minutes after the LAST edit, restarting on every further edit, no cap** (user decision; `CHANGE_HOLD_MIN=30`, so with the 10-min scheduler tick the alert lands 30-40 min after the last edit; `first_at` column is now unused); (3) **many entries entered in one sitting go out as ONE message per person** ("Calendar updates", up to 15 lines): every queued change slides the timer of all held changes, so they flush together (`notify_batch`); a single change keeps the detailed format; (4) non-Latin titles (Hindi etc.) can't be drawn by the available fonts, so the **pictures** show the kind name ("Public holiday") for those -- text and email keep the real title (`_img_label`); (5) `* _ ~ ``` stripped from titles (`_wa_safe`) so WhatsApp formatting can't break; (6) **approved leave is now in the digest** (names only, never the reason; half days marked; weekdays only; text, email and the week picture, not the month picture); (7) picture rows expire after 72 h instead of 24 h (plain messages still 24 h); (8) picture links: email needs an `<img>` URL that loads without login, so they stay unlisted-not-private -- mitigations: 24-byte random token, no personal data besides names on leave, stored pictures deleted after 14 days. A truly private option would be attaching the pictures to the email instead of linking them (heavier mail, some clients block it) -- not done. The emoji legend line under the weekly text was removed (2026-10-12, user request; the pictures still have their own legend). **Still true:** one WhatsApp per person per flush; two employees sharing one number get doubles; picture messages are dropped after 72 h if the laptop stays off.
148. **Attendance Excel now reads HR's office calendar -- 2026-10-12.** `GET /api/attendance/export-sheets` (`routes/attendance.py`) loads `office_calendar` (holiday / wfh / office; events ignored) into `cal_by_day`. (1) A **public holiday** on a weekday with no check-in is now Day Type **"Holiday"** (light-blue fill) instead of "Leave", for every employee: it is not counted in Leaves, Working Days or Attendance %, and gets no credit either way (before this, a day like 2 Oct showed as Leave and dragged % down). Someone who did check in on a holiday keeps the normal Full/Half Day label. (2) New **Work Mode** column (J) on the per-employee sheets and the "All" sheet: "Holiday: <name>", "Work from home", "In-person office" (combined with " / " if both are set). (3) Monthly Summary and Total rows gained **Holidays** and **WFH Days** columns (L, M). (4) The Summary sheet legend/notes explain Holiday and Work Mode. WFH/office days do not change Day Type (a WFH day with no check-in is still an absence). Leave balance already skipped holidays (#146), unchanged. The calendar is read live at export time, so editing it changes past rows too. Verified with a temp-DB test (holiday labelled, WFH day worked, no Leave counted, work-mode text, month Holidays/WFH counts, working days exclude the holiday); not opened in real Excel.
149. **MMGA organisation knowledge loaded -- 2026-10-12, `backend/org_profile.py` + `docs/mmga-os.md`.** The founders' "MMGA OS V2" document (roles, DM structure, services, operating rhythm, SOP rules, SMM 3-week cycle, Lumina's planned automations) is stored three ways: (1) full text `docs/mmga-os.md` (proposals in the source are marked as proposals), also uploaded to the bot's knowledge base as `mmga-os.md` via `/api/storage/doc` (so `search_knowledge_base` finds it; re-upload the same filename to replace); (2) structured tables `org_people` and `org_projects` (created and seeded by `org_profile._ensure()` only when empty, so edits made in the database survive deploys; `find_project(name)` does a loose client lookup); (3) a ~680-word `prompt_block()` injected into the **employee** system prompt (inside the cached block, never for clients) so the bot knows who owns what without a search. Mapping used: KG = Kshitij (emp004), VG = Vidit (emp001), Charu = Charulata (emp007), Saurav = emp012; Meet and Shums have no employee accounts. **Clients/DMs as of the document:** OMOTEC and Agrius = 360 Marketing, DM Charu + VG; Mellow and 1Founder = only SMM, DM Charu; TopGreen and Bothra = branding, DM Nupur; websites = Nupur + Abhinav; MMGA Production = Happy + Saurav; performance layer of OMOTEC/Agrius/Mellow = Meet + Shums. **(Built later the same day, see #150)** the "what is happening with <client>" card (SMM status, posts this/next week, production, client feedback, blocker, DM, Needs VG); the existing `get_client_status` tool is unchanged. The document's planned Lumina automations (Mon 10 AM plan count, Mon 3 PM reminder, 5:30 PM approval list, 7:30 PM daily update) are only partly present (existing: 7:30 PM EOD personal DM, Friday weekly wrap, daily approvals via the PA brief); not changed. Verified with a temp-DB test (block content, loose lookup, seed-once, employee prompt has it, client prompt does not).
150. **Client status card + MMGA rhythm messages -- 2026-10-12.** (1) **Card:** `backend/client_card.py`, used by the bot tool `get_client_status` (employee only; the old free-text version is gone) and a quick path in `_fast_standup_command` (`_CLIENT_Q`: "what is happening with Mellow", "status on Omotec", "how is X", "where are we with X" -- only for names in `org_profile`, anything else goes to the model). Fixed layout, built from live data, copied exactly by the bot: SMM Active / This week: N posts (n live) / Next week: N posts planned / Production: N pending / Client feedback: N open / Blocker / DM / Needs VG. Definitions: posts = social tasks (service "Social Media" or a [Story|Static|Reel|Carousel|Post|Video] title) by Post Day; This week = Mon-Sun now, Next week = following Mon-Sun; live = Posted/Final; **Production pending** = next week's posts not yet Scheduled/Approved/Posted/Final/Need for approval; **Client feedback open** = client-portal feedback (`client_task_feedback`) on still-open tasks that is "changes requested" or a comment, approvals not counted; **Blocker** = standup blockers of the last 3 days whose task matches a client task; **SMM Active** = posts within 14 days back or 30 days ahead; **Needs VG = Yes (...)** when anything is waiting for approval OR overdue (user decision). Branding/CRM/website clients get a project layout (open tasks, next deadline, feedback, blocker, DM, Needs VG). DM comes from `org_profile`. Preview: `GET /api/companion/client-card?client=Mellow`. (2) **Org change:** Mastek is a **CRM** project with **Abhinav as the only DM** (seed version 3 migrates existing rows; edited rows are never overwritten; the generic "Websites" row is gone). (3) **Rhythm messages** (`backend/ops_rhythm.py`, scheduler job every 5 min, WhatsApp via `wa_outbox`, once per day per kind via `app_settings` key `rhythm_<kind>_<date>`, skipped on weekends, office-calendar holidays and for people on approved leave): **Mon 10:00-11:00 Weekly Planning** ("You have N pieces to plan this week" = social posts dated two weeks ahead, per recurring client; to each client's DM(s) and to Vidit for all), **Mon 15:00 reminder** ("Your 4 PM planning meeting starts in 1 hour. Please update your planned deliverables and blockers.", to every DM and Vidit; the existing group reminder is unchanged), **Mon-Fri 17:30 6 PM Review** (items at Need for approval, to Vidit for all clients and to each DM for theirs; nothing sent when there are none). The document's 7:30 PM "today's update" already exists as the EOD personal DM. Test sends: `POST /api/companion/rhythm/run?kind=planning|reminder|approvals&to=emp003` (`to` required). Verified with a temp-DB test using a fake Notion (card numbers, feedback, blockers, both Needs VG reasons, branding/CRM layouts, fast path, every message's recipients and windows, once-a-day, holiday, leave, forced test); **Checked against real Notion data the same day, three fixes:** Notion calls the client "1 FOUNDER" (matching now ignores spaces/punctuation in both org_profile.find_project and the bot's _find_client); OMOTEC and Agrius showed 62 and 142 overdue because of old unfinished tasks, so **overdue now means due within the last 14 days** (OVERDUE_DAYS) and older unfinished items appear on a separate "Old backlog: N" line that does not set Needs VG; a client that exists in the org table but not in Notion (Mastek) answers "Not in the CRM yet" with its DM. Notion clients that exist today: Omotec, 1 FOUNDER, Shyygl, MMGA, Agrius, Mellow, TopGreen (no Bothra, no Mastek; Shyygl and MMGA are not in the MMGA OS document, so they have no DM yet). Mellow's real data has no posts dated this week or next, so its card correctly says "No posts scheduled". Not yet seen on a phone.
151. **Natural-command coverage + management views -- 2026-10-12.** The user listed the commands Lumina should understand (tasks, projects, people, approvals, management). Audit result: already handled -- add/complete/assign task (`add_standup_task`/`create_task`/`update_standup_task`/`assign_task`), my tasks and "what is pending" (`get_my_tasks`), what is due this week (`get_task_schedule`), what is X working on (`get_teammate_tasks`), what's happening with a client (#150), what's waiting for approval and approve/reject (`get_pending_approvals`/`review_task`). **New:** (1) `backend/mgmt_views.py` + tool `get_management_view` (views `blocked`, `at_risk`, `needs_vg`, `completed_week`, `overloaded`, `waiting_on_me`, `my_approvals`), all built in code from live data and copied by the bot exactly. Definitions: **blocked** = clients with a standup blocker (last 3 days) on one of their tasks; **at risk** = per client, score from overdue (due in the last 14 days; older ignored, same rule as the card), posts due within 3 days not yet ready, blockers (x3), open client feedback, worst six first with the DM; **needs VG** = clients with anything waiting for approval or overdue, with the waiting titles; **completed this week** = distinct standup tasks marked done Mon-today per person plus posts Posted/Final dated this week per client; **overloaded** = per person standup tasks pending today + Notion tasks assigned to them due today to +7 days; flagged when at least max(8, 1.5 x the average of people who have any work); recently overdue (last 14 days) is shown separately as "overdue" and people with 5 or more get a "Most behind" line (first version counted overdue as load and made everyone look equally overloaded on the real data); **waiting on me** = approvals for the asker's clients (everything for Vidit), standup tasks handed to them by others still open, and other people's blockers whose text names them; **my approvals** = Vidit sees every item waiting for approval, a DM sees their clients' (via `ops_rhythm._clients_by_person`), anyone else sees none. (2) **`reschedule_task {task, to}`** ("move X to Friday", "delay X to Monday"): the person's own standup task gets that due date (also on Notion) and, if the date is after today, moves to that day's standup (it leaves today's list and the reply says so and shows the updated list); past dates, impossible dates and dates over a year away are refused; audited as a write tool. (3) **Approvals no longer guess:** `review_task` uses `_match_approval` (client name + title words, all words must be covered, leading zeros ignored) and asks "more than one fits" on a tie instead of approving the first; "Approve OMOTEC Reel 03" resolves to that reel when it is unique. (4) **Quick paths without the model** (`_MGMT_FAST`, `_DUE_WEEK`, `_PENDING_Q` in `_fast_standup_command`): "what is at risk", "what needs VG", "show me all blocked projects", "who is overloaded", "who is waiting on me", "what needs my approval", "what did we complete this week", "what is due this week", "what is pending". Phrases outside these (e.g. "approve ...", "delay ...") go to the model, which has the new tools and a prompt line mapping them. (5) `client_card.gather_row(c)` split out of `gather(name)` so views reuse a resolved client. Preview any view with real data: `GET /api/companion/mgmt-view?view=at_risk&user_id=emp003` (token). Verified with a temp-DB test using a fake Notion (every view, approval ties and zero-padding, reschedule happy path and refusals, client lockout, fast paths); real-data check after deploy is in the session notes, not a guarantee about the model's tool choice on other phrasings.
152. **Test pass on #149-#151 (card, management views, reschedule, rhythm) -- 2026-10-12.** Three suites were run: a phrasing battery for the quick-reply matching (about 60 messages, both what must shortcut and what must go to the model), hostile-input tests (Notion off or raising, garbage field types, duplicate/None client names, blank and unicode input, schedulers with Notion down, someone with no WhatsApp number, VG on leave) and the earlier functional suites. **Flaws found and fixed:** (1) the client-card shortcut fired on fragments and on longer questions: "how is ma" matched Mastek, "MMGA" matched MMGA Production, "what is happening with the omotec shoot on friday" returned the generic Omotec card instead of an answer about the shoot. `org_profile.find_project` is now exact-first, then a client name (4+ letters) inside the query, then the query inside a client name only when it is most of it (min 3 letters); the shortcut additionally requires the phrase to be (nearly) just the client name ("omotec", "omotec ltd"); "whats" without an apostrophe now works. (2) A standup task that is not linked to Notion but names the client ("Mellow product shoot edit") now counts as that client's blocker (card, blocked, at risk). (3) `reschedule_task` refuses an already-done task, and warns when moving your only task leaves today's standup empty (Lumina's standup lock would otherwise catch you by surprise). (4) The 10:00 planning and 17:30 approvals messages released their once-a-day claim if the CRM could not be read, so the next 5-minute tick retries instead of silently skipping the day. (5) A task with a non-text status or title crashed the card and the approval matcher; both coerce to text now. (6) Every management view re-read all clients from Notion (3 s, 10 s cold on real data): `mgmt_views._all_clients` is cached for 45 s, never caches an empty result, and is cleared by any write tool (`_run_tool` calls `mgmt_views.invalidate()` for names in `_WRITE_TOOLS`), so an approval shows immediately. **Known, left on purpose:** `reschedule_task` on a social post also moves its Notion due date, which is the Post Day (a delay of the work and of the publish date are the same thing here); "move X to <person>" is a hand-over (`delegate_my_task`), not a reschedule; approvals are open to every employee (the document says VG/DM approve, but nothing restricts it yet); "what is pending" means your own open tasks, not approvals; group answers to "what needs my approval" are visible to the whole group; the model's choice of tool for phrasings outside the shortcuts was NOT tested (no model key locally).
153. **The assistant thinks for itself -- 2026-10-12.** User: it should behave like a human PA, be very smart, answer any question with its own mind, not only from the database. What was holding it back, and what changed in `whatsapp_agent.py` / `web_search.py`: (1) **Prompt:** a new `_BRAIN` block (own knowledge and judgement for general questions, writing, strategy, pricing, maths, tech and advice; tools only for MMGA facts; give an opinion and one recommendation with a reason and the main risk; disagree politely; add one line about a related problem it noticed; ask a clarifying question only when truly stuck; sound like a person). The old rule "if nothing has the answer, say so in one line" is replaced by "say what's missing in one line, then still help with your own reasoning or a search". The 1 to 3 sentence cap now only applies to chit-chat and quick facts; real questions get a complete answer (usually under 200 words, longer for drafts and plans). Persona rules (reverent to Abhinav, dry for others) are unchanged. (2) **Web search works on any model:** the Claude-only hosted `web_search` tool (useless while on Qwen) is replaced by a function tool `search_web` backed by `web_search.answer()`: OpenRouter's web plugin (one small call that searches and writes a short sourced answer, about $0.02 per search, recorded in the usage dashboard) with a DuckDuckGo HTML fallback. Employees only. The tool array's `cache_control` marker moved to this last tool. (3) **Stronger model for hard questions:** `_needs_deep_thinking` (advice, explain, compare, draft/write, strategy, "should we", "what should I focus on", "how do I handle...", any message over 160 characters; a short command that starts with add/assign/approve/etc. or is about "my standup" with no question mark is NOT deep) sends the message to **Claude Sonnet** (falls back to Qwen if Claude fails) with `max_tokens` 1400; everything else stays on Qwen with 900 (was 600). Cost: a Sonnet message is roughly 2 to 5 cents with caching versus about a tenth of a cent on Qwen, so it is capped: **40 smart messages per day** (`WHATSAPP_SMART_DAILY_CAP`, counter in `app_settings` key `wa_smart_<date>`); switch it off with env `WHATSAPP_SMART=off` or `app_settings whatsapp_smart_llm=off`; it is also off when there is no `ANTHROPIC_API_KEY`. Clients never get it. The no-tool guard (#142) no longer fires on deep questions. (4) `_humanize` now turns `**bold**` into WhatsApp's `*bold*` and strips `#` headings, since the stronger model writes markdown. (5) **Live check route:** `GET /api/companion/ask-test?q=...&user_id=emp003` (token) runs one question through the real agent and returns the reply plus which tier answered; nothing is sent to WhatsApp, the person's saved context is restored, but it does use the model, search and the daily smart counter. **Verified live:** the pricing question used the real Omotec data and gave a recommendation (Sonnet, 16 s); a Mellow caption request produced three options with a pick; "what is happening with Instagram Reels reach" searched and cited sources on Qwen; "what is the capital of France" stayed on the cheap model (2 s); "add buy milk to my standup" still goes straight through the fast path. Known: smart answers take 8 to 17 seconds; web results can be a year old (the answer says which year); quality of the cheap model on borderline questions that do not match the detector is Qwen's, so extend `_DEEP` if some phrasing should always use the stronger model.
154. **6 PM review off, whole-day group summaries, one-tap check-in link -- 2026-10-12.** (1) The Mon-Fri 17:30 approvals review message (`ops_rhythm`) is switched off by removing `approvals` from `_WINDOWS` (the code stays; re-add the line to turn it back on; planning Mon 10:00 and the Mon 15:00 reminder are unchanged). (2) `group_memory.search_text` with no query now returns the WHOLE window (up to 800 messages / 22000 chars, each message cut to 240 chars) instead of 40, so "summarise the group today" works with 100+ messages; a keyword search returns up to 80 matches / 6000 chars; if it has to drop the oldest it says how many ("ask for a shorter time range"). The always-attached context when someone addresses the bot in the group is still the last 40 messages (3000 chars); anything older comes through `read_group_chat`. (3) **`frontend/checkin.html`**: opening it checks the logged-in person in (or out with `?a=out`) with their browser location, one tap plus "Allow" (it posts to the same `/api/attendance/checkin` as the dashboard; location is optional and a denial still checks in). It is exempt from the standup-lock redirect in `auth.js` (like `standup.html`), and the login-nudge DM (`job_login_nudge`) now includes `<url>/checkin.html` when check-in is missing. Not seen on a real phone. Scripts-only on the laptop (self-update), no bridge restart.
155. **Group answers are built in code, not free-written -- 2026-10-12, `backend/group_digest.py`.** A model summarising the group invented quotes ("Happy said omotec is not working", said by nobody) and missed real topics. So now: (1) "what was discussed / summarise / recap ... group" (needs a group word plus a discuss/summarise/said word; handled before the fast paths in `handle_message`) is answered by `group_digest.build()` with no model: header (count, time range, who spoke how often), then every stored message verbatim as `HH:MM *Name*: text` (links shown as `[link: domain]`, long texts cut with "..."), plus a note when recording started after the window began. Windows: today (default), yesterday, last N hours/days, this week; a first name in the question filters to that person; the word "client" filters to messages naming a known client. (2) Over 45 messages (or too long) it switches to topic view: the model may ONLY group message numbers into topics (temperature-free JSON, cheap model); code writes each topic header from the data (counts, speakers, times, first and last real message quoted), a model title that is not made of words found in the topic's messages is replaced by a quote of its first line, unassigned messages go to "Other"; if the model fails it shows the latest 45 verbatim and says so. (3, revised) Safety net for the free-form path: a wrong quote no longer replaces the answer with "I couldn't verify my own summary" (users kept seeing that). Now the model gets ONE retry ("these quoted phrases are not in the log, rewrite using only the log, paraphrase instead of quoting"); if that is clean it is used, else the checked summary of the real messages is shown with no apology. Original text of (3): safety net for the free-form path (`read_group_chat` through the model, e.g. "what did Charu say about Mellow"): the tool output is remembered in `turn["group_log"]` and every quoted passage (12+ chars) in the model's reply must exist in it (`quotes_ok`), otherwise the reply is replaced by "I couldn't verify my own summary, so here are the real messages" plus the verbatim digest. **Update, same day: the default is now a short CHECKED summary, verbatim only on request.** `group_digest._summary` has the stronger model (Sonnet within the daily cap, else Qwen) write 4-9 points as JSON `{text, ids}` where ids are the message numbers that prove the point; code then verifies each point (`_point_ok`): it must cite real ids, contain no hedge words (probably/likely/seems/maybe...), every capitalised name and number must appear in the cited messages, and quoted text must exist. A word-by-word match turned out too strict (it rejected faithful paraphrases like "wearing"/"themed"), so the content check is an independent fact-checker call (`_verify`: each claim plus only its cited messages, "supported: true/false"); the word match is only the fallback if that call fails. Debug what the writer said and what was rejected with `GET /api/companion/group-summary-debug?token=` (`group_digest.LAST`). Rejected points are dropped; if more are bad than good (or the call fails) the verbatim view is shown. Each point ends with the time of its first cited message. Say "exact messages from the group" (also "full chat", "word for word") for the verbatim view. Trade-off: very paraphrased Hinglish points tend to be rejected, which is intentional (accuracy over fluency). Limits: topic titles/links are the only non-verbatim parts of the verbatim view; the bot cannot know anything said before recording began (from 14:20 on 2026-10-12; five earlier messages were imported by hand from a paste).
156. **Permanent message archive lives on the laptop, Railway keeps only a 7-day working copy -- 2026-10-12.** `whatsapp-bridge/index.js` (`archive()`) appends every message the bot reads to `whatsapp-bridge/archive/YYYY-MM-DD.jsonl` (IST day; one JSON object per line: `ts`, `at`, `dir` in/out, `chat` group/dm, group id/name, `from`, `name`, `id`, `text`): allow-listed groups (everything said there), DMs to the bot, and every reply the bot sends. Voice notes and images of allow-listed groups are saved to `archive/media/` (`<date>_<id>.<ext>`) with a `type`/`caption`/`file` record. Groups that are NOT allow-listed are never archived (the bot ignores them). The folder is gitignored. On Railway, `group_memory.RETENTION_DAYS` dropped 30 -> 7, so the database only holds what the bot needs to answer ("today", "this week") and the volume stops growing; summaries older than 7 days are no longer possible from Railway (the full text is on the laptop). **Not built:** answering from the laptop archive (Railway cannot reach the laptop), backing the folder up off the machine (use `--backup-mirror` style copy or a cloud-synced folder if wanted), and archiving anything that happened before this change. The bridge change takes effect when the laptop's self-update restarts the bridge.
157. **Role-based morning dashboard -- 2026-10-12, `backend/morning_dash.py`.** Replaces the boss-only `pa_brief` morning send (the scheduler job `_run_pa_brief` now calls `morning_dash.run_daily`; `pa_brief` stays for the on-demand `get_my_brief`). Built entirely in code from live data (no model, so every number is exact) and sent through `wa_outbox` once per person per weekday 09:00-11:00 IST (claim key `morning_dash_<date>_<emp>`, released on failure so the next 10-min tick retries; skipped on weekends, public holidays, approved leave, no WhatsApp number). Three layouts: **leader** (`LEADERS` = emp001 VG, emp004 KG, emp003 Abhinav): "Today's priorities" (client counts red/amber/green: red = risk score >= 8 or a blocker plus overdue, amber = any risk score, green = none, using `mgmt_views._risk_reasons`), meetings, decisions waiting (approvals per client + pending leave for approvers), today's client deadlines, blocked clients, who is on leave; Abhinav also gets his own work block. **DM** (anyone who owns a client in `org_profile`): the same but only for their own clients, plus their own work. **Member** (designers, editors, HR...): meetings, their own work (red = overdue in the last 14 days, amber = due today, green = due in 7 days, standup done/to-do, the first four things to start, their own blockers) and nothing about the business. Noorish also sees her pending leave decisions; leaders and HR see who is on leave. Meetings come from HR's office-calendar events plus the fixed rhythm (Mon 4 PM Weekly Planning, Wed/Fri 4 PM Team meeting, Tue/Thu 10:30 AM Class meeting); the line "Today:" shows a WFH / office / holiday mark. The old "6 PM Approval" meeting is not listed (that review is switched off). On demand: "my dashboard", "what's my day", "morning brief" (fast path, no model) or tool `get_my_dashboard`. Preview without sending: `GET /api/companion/morning-dash?user_id=emp001` (token); force-send to specific ids: `POST /api/companion/morning-dash/run?to=emp003`. **Update:** the personal block for members/DMs/Abhinav is now driven by today's standup: a pending task hanging 2+ days (rolled over via `carried_from`, or past its `due_date`) is red/urgent, hanging exactly 1 day is amber/at risk, anything newer is green; urgent ones are listed with their days, and a separate "On the board" line shows Notion tasks due today / overdue in the last 14 days. Not done: google calendar meetings (parked), per-person meeting lists beyond the fixed ones, showing it in the web app.
158. **Callouts (@mentions) get names, count as "asks", and the people who made them are reminded -- 2026-10-12.** (1) **Names, not numbers:** group messages tag people as `@<LID digits>`; the bridge (`resolveMentionText`) now swaps a LID for the phone number when WhatsApp's LID map knows it, and `group_memory.resolve_mentions` turns `@<digits>` into `@Name` (employee phone numbers, last 10 digits, plus a manual map in `app_settings` key `wa_lid_alias`) when storing and summarising. For numbers it can't resolve, teach it: `POST /api/companion/lid-alias?digits=71884968308912&name=Charulata` (token), which also rewrites already-stored messages (done for Charulata). Two other numbers seen so far (`217230889476239`, `47124582502549`) are still unknown. (2) **Summary:** the writer is told a message that tags or calls out a named person with a request is important and must be its own point (who asked whom to do what); anything not covered is still listed word for word. (3) **Asks tracker:** the group extractor (`group_intel`) now also returns `asks` (`from`, `to`, action, due, exact quote; same quote-must-exist check, `to` must be a real teammate) stored in `group_commitments` with new columns `requester_name`/`requester_id` (empty for self-promises). The person called out is nudged by the existing end-of-day follow-up flow. (4) **Requesters are reminded:** `group_intel.run_asks_digest` (scheduler job `asks_digest`, every 10 min, acts once per weekday 17:00-18:30 IST via claim key `asks_digest_<date>_<emp>`) DMs each requester (`app_settings asks_digest_ids`, default `emp001,emp004` = Vidit and Kshitij) a list of the asks they made that still look unfinished: before listing, each ask is checked against the owner's done standup tasks and Notion (`_standup_evidence`/`_notion_evidence`) and silently closed if evidence exists; the DM says to reply "close #<n>" to close one. Nothing is sent when nothing is open. **Also (same day):** the 19:30 EOD DM to Vidit and Kshitij (`leads_text` of `/api/companion/eod-summary`) now uses the same layout as the 17:00 group post (`*EOD check: X/Y tasks done today*`, then each person with `✅ title - done` / `❌ title - not done`, \"No standup yet\" line), with the raise/holiday jab kept for anyone with more than 5 done. Limits: only messages that arrive after this deploy (and are not yet processed) are scanned for asks, so the 12 Oct Stempedia/Twinkle messages will not appear unless re-added with `add_group_commitment`; mentions in older messages stay as numbers unless an alias is set; the bridge change needs a bridge restart (self-update).
159. **Old made-up group answers kept coming back from chat memory -- fixed 2026-10-12.** After the summary fix the bot still replied "Happy saying lumina is not working..." to "tell me the important things" / "tell me the things of the group and what is pressing": those wordings did not match the group-question detector, so they went to the model, which copied the fabricated summaries sitting in the saved chat memory (7 days, 10 turns). Fixes: (1) the one-time wipe of `whatsapp_agent_context` (flag `ctx_reset_20261012`; only the bot's short-term chat memory); (2) an answer built from the group chat is never saved word for word, a stub is saved instead (`turn["group_log"]`), so later turns can't copy it; (3) the group-question detector is looser (`group_digest._LOOSE`: group word plus things/tell me/update/news/pressing/important/urgent/anything/missed/going on...; and without a group word only "what did I miss", "catch me up", "anything important/new/pressing"); (4) "pressing / important / urgent / what did I miss" questions put the tracked open promises and asks first ("Open in the group"), then the checked summary; (5) the prompt says never reuse an earlier group answer. A bare "summary" / "give me a summary" / "summarise" / "recap" (`group_digest._BARE`) also means the group chat (it used to fall to the model and return a tasks rundown in 30 s); the reply ends with a hint that "my dashboard" is the personal overview. Group digests also (a) hide anything that follows password/pass/pwd/OTP/PIN/secret/token/api key (`group_digest.redact`, applied to verbatim lines and summary points; a Canva login was shared in the group) and (b) leave out messages that are just people talking to the bot ("lumina give me summary...", bare summary requests). **Format (revised):** the default reply is now ONLY the checked points under a one-line header (`*Group summary, today* (N messages)`): no times, no "Also in the chat" dump, no recording-start note, no footer, no hint line. Messages the first pass did not cover get their own second writer+checker pass (`_gen`) so nothing real is left out; the exact messages appear only when asked ("exact messages"). Known gap: a bare "tell me the important things" with no group word still goes to the model (it now has a clean memory, the tool and the quote guard).
160. **Quality over cost: the WhatsApp assistant runs on the best model for everything -- 2026-10-12 (owner: "idc how much it costs, I want best").** (1) Every message that reaches a model (employees AND clients, no more `_needs_deep_thinking` gate; fast paths and the code-built group digest still skip the model) uses Claude first with a fallback chain `WHATSAPP_BEST_MODELS` (default `claude-opus-5-5,claude-sonnet-5-5,claude-sonnet-4-6`): each call tries the chain from the first model that has worked so far in this process (`_BEST_START`), so a model the key can't use is skipped, then Qwen via OpenRouter, then plain Claude. The model that actually answered is written back to the usage record (billed at the sonnet rate, an under-estimate if Opus answers). (2) `WHATSAPP_SMART_DAILY_CAP` default is now 0 = no cap (the off switches `WHATSAPP_SMART=off` / `app_settings whatsapp_smart_llm=off` still work). (3) The WhatsApp assistant ignores the monthly spend limit (`whatsapp_agent.check_budget_available` returns allowed unless `WHATSAPP_ENFORCE_BUDGET=1`); the web chat's own budget gate is unchanged. (4) Bigger limits: replies up to 2500 tokens, 12 tool rounds (was 6), 80 recent group messages / 6000 chars in a group (was 40 / 3000). (5) The group promise/ask extractor (`group_intel`) now runs on Claude by default (`GROUP_INTEL_LLM=qwen` to go back), as do the group summary writer and fact-checker (they already used the smart path). Qwen remains the fallback only. `GET /api/companion/ask-test` now reports the real model name (`model`) and tier that answered. Check the Anthropic bill and `/api/companion/ask-test` after deploy to see which model in the chain the key can use.
161. **Revised #160 the same day: Qwen uses only its top models, Claude goes back to the earlier setup -- 2026-10-12.** Owner: "for qwen only use high models, claude keep the earlier ones". (1) **Qwen chain** (`llm_openrouter.chain()`): `qwen/qwen3.8-max-0902, qwen/qwen3.6-plus, qwen/qwen3.8-27b` (first version used `qwen/qwen3-max`, which OpenRouter reports deprecated on 9 Oct 2026 and points to `qwen3.8-max-0902`; the chain skipped it by itself) (override with `OPENROUTER_MODELS`; `OPENROUTER_MODEL` if set is tried last); each call starts at the first model that has worked in this process and walks down on any error, so an unavailable slug is skipped automatically; `lo.create(..., model="slug")` pins one (used by `GET /api/companion/llm-status?test=1&model=qwen/qwen3-max`, which also lists `qwen_chain`). Default price estimate in `MODEL_COSTS["qwen"]` is now $1.20 in / $6.00 out per 1M (qwen3-max; set `OPENROUTER_PRICE_IN/OUT` if different). qwen3-max supports explicit prompt caching (in `_CACHE_MODELS`). (2) **Claude is back to the earlier arrangement:** normal messages go to Qwen (Claude Haiku only as its fallback), thinking-heavy messages (`_needs_deep_thinking`) go to `claude-sonnet-4-6` (`WHATSAPP_BEST_MODELS` default is just that), the group promise/ask extractor is back on Qwen unless `GROUP_INTEL_LLM=claude`. (3) **Kept from #160** because they were about cost, not model choice: no daily cap on smart messages (`WHATSAPP_SMART_DAILY_CAP` default 0), the WhatsApp assistant ignores the monthly spend limit (`WHATSAPP_ENFORCE_BUDGET=1` to enforce), 2500-token replies, 12 tool rounds, 80 group messages of context.
162. **Group summary is split by topic with an Important block -- 2026-10-13.** The writer now returns a `topic` (2-3 words) and `important` flag per point; `group_digest._summary` shows `*❗ Important*` first (requests/deadlines aimed at a named person, decisions, client asks, approvals, blockers: the model's judgement, only emphasis, every point is still fact-checked), then one `*Topic*` section per topic in time order. Topic names come from the model and are not fact-checked (labels only). Group days run 3 AM to the next 3 AM IST (group_digest._window, DAY_START_HOUR): default = since the last 3 AM, "yesterday" or a named day (monday, 12 oct, 12/10) = that 3 AM-to-3 AM block, "this week" from Monday 3 AM, "last N hours/days" unchanged. The raw-message view is gone: even 'exact messages' returns a summary; if a summary fails it is retried twice, then says so (no dump), and a partly-rejected summary keeps its good points. Qwen chain is now qwen3.8-27b then qwen3.6-plus as backup, never Max (owner decision 2026-10-13; chain, default and cost estimate $0.045/$2.30 updated; set OPENROUTER_MODELS on Railway if one is set there, it overrides). The 9:30 AM / 6:30 PM tomorrow-live DM now defaults to Vidit AND Charulata (content_calendar_recipient_ids default emp001,emp007; a stored setting replaces it). WhatsApp layout: the 4:55 PM and 7:30 PM open-task DMs now list one numbered task per line under a bold heading (laptop_agent._task_lines), the 10:30/11 PM nudges and follow-up fallback have line breaks, and the bot's system prompt has a LAYOUT rule (one item per line, bold heading, blank line between sections). Calendar attendance dots: 9h+ worked = full day (was 6h; leave_store.calendar_days).
163. **Alexa skill: talk to the Lumina assistant by voice -- 2026-10-13, `routes/alexa.py`, `alexa_link.py`, `alexa_verify.py`.** Skill "Lumina" (id `amzn1.ask.skill.412f64ef-efbb-4137-ae60-d08b9baa9f8e`, the code default; `ALEXA_SKILL_ID` env overrides), English (India) only (the console offers no Hindi for this account; Hinglish words work inside English), invocation `lumina`, custom model, own hosting. (1) **Endpoint `POST /api/alexa`**: checks Amazon's signature (`alexa_verify`: cert URL must be https://s3.amazonaws.com/echo.api/..., leaf in date and for echo-api.amazon.com, chain trusted via certifi using `cryptography.x509.verification` -- added `cryptography>=43` to requirements.txt -- and the RSA signature over the raw body; `ALEXA_VERIFY_SIGNATURE=0` turns it off for debugging only), the skill id and the request timestamp (150 s). Then it calls the SAME `whatsapp_agent.handle_message()` as the WhatsApp bot with the employee's WhatsApp number, so tools, data, memory, fast paths and the chat context are shared; new kwarg `channel="voice"` adds a prompt block asking for 1-4 spoken sentences. (2) **No transcript from Alexa**, only intents: a catch-all `AMAZON.SearchQuery` slot needs a leading carrier word that Alexa drops, so every carrier in `CARRIERS` (what, show me, add, mark, approve, mere, kya, batao...) is its own intent `Q_<slug>` and the word is put back in front of the slot text. Also `LinkIntent` (AMAZON.NUMBER), `AnswerIntent`, Yes/No (feed "yes"/"no" to the bot, so confirm-before-send works), Help/Stop/Cancel/Fallback. The model JSON is served at `GET /api/alexa/interaction-model` and saved in `deploy/alexa/interaction-model.en-IN.json`; edit `CARRIERS` and re-paste it in the console when phrasing needs widening. (3) **Speech**: `to_speech()` strips WhatsApp formatting, reads ✅/❌ as done/not done, links as "a link", reads at most 5 list lines (+"and N more") and ~650 chars; when shortened, the full reply goes to the person's WhatsApp (`wa_outbox`). The Alexa app card gets the full text. (4) **Long answers**: the assistant runs in a thread; if not done in 6 s (`WAIT_SECONDS`, Alexa gives up at ~8 s) Alexa says "give me a moment, then say what's the answer", the result is stored in `alexa_pending` (30 min) and also sent to WhatsApp; "what's the answer" (AnswerIntent or Q_what_s) reads it. A lock makes sure a reply finishing at the cut-off is never lost. (5) **Who is talking**: tables `alexa_links` (key `person:<voice profile id>` when Alexa sends one, else `user:<amazon account id>`), `alexa_link_codes`, `alexa_link_fails`. On WhatsApp an employee sends "link my alexa" (DM) -> 6-digit code valid 10 min -> "Alexa, tell Lumina code 1 2 3 4 5 6" (or "my code is ..." in words). Codes are single-use; 5 wrong codes per account per hour or 30 overall per 10 min lock linking. "unlink my alexa" removes their links. A left/inactive employee is treated as unlinked. (6) **Status**: `GET /api/companion/alexa-status?token=` shows skill id, signature check on/off, whether cryptography is available, the last request/error and who is linked. Verified: 49-check scratch suite with a temp DB and a fake assistant (model validity, rejects wrong skill/stale/unsigned, linking + single use + lockout + voice profile + left employee, carrier put back, voice channel, speech cleanup, list shortening + WhatsApp copy, deferral/still-working/collect, Yes/No, verify URL rules, signature math, self-signed chain refused) and the chain check against Amazon's real (2023) echo-api chain as of a valid date. **Not verified against a live Alexa request** (needs the JSON pasted and the endpoint set in the console).
164. **What's-new announcements -- 2026-10-14, `backend/releases.py`, `routes/releases.py`, `frontend/whatsnew.js`, `scripts/add_release.py`, `config/releases.json`.** Owner standing instruction: every new feature gets (1) a short WhatsApp to everyone and (2) a popup in Lumina when each person next opens it. (1) **One file drives both**: `config/releases.json` is a list of `{id, date, title, bullets[], notify?}`; `scripts/add_release.py` appends an entry (id = date + slug). (2) **Announcer**: `releases.sync()` runs on Railway about a minute after every boot (so right after a deploy) and every 3 minutes (`task_scheduler._run_release_sync`). An entry whose id is not in table `release_log` is inserted FIRST (that is the claim, so overlapping runs cannot both send), then announced once: a WhatsApp via `wa_outbox` to every active employee with a number (the laptop delivers it). Outside 09:00-21:00 IST the message is held until 09:30 (`send_after`). Text = `*What's new in Lumina*`, the title, up to 5 bullets (cut at a word at 170 chars), then a closing line. `"notify": false` = popup only. (3) **Popup**: `auth.js` loads `whatsnew.js` right after sign-in on every protected page except `checkin.html`; it calls `GET /api/whatsnew` (identity = the session cookie, never a body field), shows releases that person has not dismissed (published in the last 30 days, newest first, at most 3) in one dialog, and records the dismissal with `POST /api/whatsnew/seen` (table `release_seen`) on Got it / Escape / click outside, so it never shows again for them. Checked at most every 2 minutes per tab (sessionStorage); any failure is silent and never blocks the page. (4) **Inspect/test** (token-gated): `GET /api/companion/releases` (file entries, what went out, how many saw each, recipient names), `POST /api/companion/releases/sync` (run now), `POST /api/companion/releases/test?slug=<id>&to=emp003` (send one entry's WhatsApp to named people only; `to` is required so it cannot reach everyone by accident; nothing is recorded as published). Each entry also carries an optional `how` string (add_release.py `--how`): shown as a "How to use it" line in the WhatsApp and a highlighted box in the popup (popup reads it from the file, so it can be added later). First entry `2026-10-14-smarter-whatsapp-assistant` (topic group summaries, 3 AM days, standup-assign WhatsApp, one-task-per-line reminders, 9h full day); the Alexa skill is deliberately NOT announced until it is set up and usable. Verified with a 24-check temp-DB suite (invalid entries skipped, only active people with a number messaged, silent entries, announced once, night hold, popup per person, dismiss, 30-day expiry, cap of 3, session-only identity, 401 without login); not yet seen on a real phone or in a browser.
165. **Attendance answers were guessing check-in times -- fixed 2026-10-14.** The bot's `get_attendance` tool listed a person who had already checked out as "(left HH:MM)" only, so "what time did Happy check in" (asked by voice on Alexa) had no data and the model invented 9:03 (real: 11:32). It now returns "(in HH:MM, left HH:MM)" for everyone and the tool description says to quote times exactly. Affects WhatsApp and Alexa alike.
166. **Alexa answers too slow (every question became "give me a moment") -- fixed 2026-10-14.** Voice (`channel="voice"`) used the thinking-heavy Sonnet path for many questions, which takes 8-17 s, past Alexa's 8 s limit. Voice now always uses the fast model (`deep` off), 450-token replies and at most 5 tool rounds; `WAIT_SECONDS` 6 -> 7; `alexa-status` shows `last.took_s` (seconds the last question took). If it still defers, read `took_s` before changing anything.
167. **Alexa still slow after #166 -- 2026-10-14.** `ask-test` showed a plain standup question taking 11 s on Haiku: the big prompt on Qwen was failing/slow and falling back. Fixes: (1) voice calls Claude Haiku FIRST (`_llm_create(claude_first=True)`, Qwen only as fallback), (2) `_SHOW_TASKS` now also matches "what is on my daily standup", "my daily standup tasks" (answered with no model at all). Finding why Qwen fails on the full prompt (it also slows WhatsApp) is not done.
168. **"Who logged in, and when" for any person and any day -- 2026-10-19, `backend/attendance_report.py`.** The bot's `get_attendance` tool used to show only today and no per-person lookup, so "what time did Nupur log in on Monday" had no data and the model invented times (it also invented "9:05 AM" for a whole week, and did its own date maths wrongly: "Friday 17 Oct" is a Saturday). Now: (1) `attendance_report.report(today, roster, from, to, person)` builds the answer in code from `daily_attendance` (only rows with a real `checkin_time` count, a heartbeat-only row is not a login): one person one day = "in 10:40, left 19:05 (8h 25m)" with "(work from home)" from HR's office calendar; one person over a range = a line per day (over 7 days: days present, then one "No login on N weekdays" line); the whole team one day = "First in / Last in" plus "Logged in (n)" and "Not logged in (n)"; team over several days = days present per person. A day with no check-in is explained when known: weekend, public holiday (name), approved leave, future date. Never more than 31 days. (2) Tool `get_attendance {person?, date?, to_date?}`: dates are parsed in CODE (`parse_when`: today, tomorrow, yesterday, monday / last monday, 16/10, 12 oct, this week, last week, this month, last month, or YYYY-MM-DD), so the model never works out dates; the system prompt also now says today's WEEKDAY. (3) No-model fast path (`_fast_attendance` in `_fast_standup_command`): "what time did X log in / check in / leave <when>", "did X come in <when>", "is X in / working today", "X's login time", "who has / hasn't logged in", "who's absent", "attendance <when>", "what time did the team log in", "who was first to log in". Anything whose period it can't read goes to the model. (4) Safety nets in `handle_message`: a login / attendance question (`_ATTENDANCE_Q`) that gets an answer without a tool call is sent back once, and if it still skips the tool the reply becomes "I can't answer that reliably..." (an invented time never goes out); the prompt says there is no official start time so nobody is called "late" unless the asker gives a cutoff; a reply in Korean/Russian/Chinese/Arabic/Thai to a Latin-script message (`_non_latin_ratio`) is sent back once to be re-answered in English (live drift seen: a Korean reply to an English question). (5) Verified: 40+ checks on a temp DB (exact times, holiday / weekend / WFH labels, heartbeat row not a login, phrase routing and non-hijacking of unrelated questions, bad dates, huge range, client refused) and live on production against the raw `/api/attendance/summary` rows (Nupur 10:35:10 today, 12 Oct 11:34-20:54 = 9h 19m, Lakshmi 12 check-in days in Oct). **If an attendance answer looks wrong**, compare with `GET /api/attendance/summary?user_id=<id>` first; the report only shows what is stored. **Related fix the same day:** `POST /api/companion/attendance-fix?token=&user_id=&date=&checkout=HH:MM` (token-gated, only edits an existing row that has a check-in, never before check-in) corrects one day's checkout, used for Abhinav's 16 Oct (the automatic heartbeat checkout at 17:05 had made a 9h+ day a Half Day). `ask-test` now returns `error_trace` when the agent fails and its spy accepts the newer `claude_first` argument. Announced to everyone in What's new (`2026-10-19-ask-lumina-who-logged-in-and-when`).
169. **Small attendance fixes, 2026-10-19 (not announced in What's new, owner's choice).** (1) The attendance Excel's per-employee Monthly Summary (and its Total row) has a last column **"OT (Work From Office)"** (col N): overtime on weekdays that are NOT marked work-from-home or holiday on HR's office calendar (weekends are not in the sheet; same rule as comp-off, #132). The existing "Overtime Hours" column still shows all overtime. (2) Calendar page (`leave.html`): a day with a rejected leave request used to lose its "Full/Half day worked" chip (an `else if`); both chips now show. (3) `POST /api/companion/attendance-fix` takes an optional `checkin=HH:MM` as well as `checkout=HH:MM` (used for Abhinav's 14 Oct: 10:30 to 20:05, and 16 Oct checkout 19:34). (4) **Full day = 5 hours (owner, 2026-10-19), overtime still after 9 hours.** Two separate numbers now: `routes/attendance.py::_FULL_DAY_MIN_HOURS = 5.0` (Full Day vs Half Day in the Excel, plus its legend) and `leave_store._FULL_DAY_HOURS = 5.0` (the Calendar's full/half dot); overtime and the comp-off conversion still use `_FULL_DAY_HOURS = 9.0` / `leave_store.OT_BASELINE_HOURS = 9.0`. Earlier gotchas that say "9 hours = full day" (#126, the 2026-10-14 What's new line) describe the OLD rule. Applies to everyone and to past dates too (it is computed at read time). Note: when a deploy is still rolling out, the OLD route can answer a call (it silently ignored `checkin`); check the response has `checkin_after` before trusting it.
173. **Calendar-change WhatsApp now also goes to the editor (supersedes #147 'except the editor') -- 2026-10-21.** Owner: Noorish (who edits the calendar) must get the update too. `week_digest.notify_change` and `notify_batch` no longer exclude the editor; the 'Updated by <name>' line stays.

174. **Group chat now creates tasks and notifies owners (`backend/group_actions.py`) -- 2026-10-21.** Owner decision: tasks explicitly asked for in the team group are created automatically and the owner is told. After each `group_intel.extract_pending` pass (actions run after the extraction transaction commits, never inside it), (a) every new **ask** aimed at a real teammate becomes a Daily Standup task for them (`delegated_from` = who asked, `group_commitments.auto_task_id` stores the row) and they get an immediate WhatsApp DM with who/what/when/quote; (b) new client **feedback** (new fact kind, with `information` also added) DMs the client's DM/co-DM (`ops_rhythm._clients_by_person`, Vidit if none). Self-promises are tracked only, not turned into tasks. Rails: no task for an ask to oneself, identical task already on that day's list not duplicated, max 6 tasks per pass, held until 09:30 outside 09:00-21:00 IST, every action in `wa_action_log` (sender `group-intel`), off switch `app_settings group_auto_actions=off`. Undo: `reject_group_commitment` also marks the created standup row deleted (the DM tells the owner to reply 'that wasn't a task #N'). Notion tasks are NOT created from group chat. Test it with `POST /api/companion/group-intel/simulate?token=` body `{messages:[{name,text}]}` (real model, writes and sends nothing; shows tasks/feedback that WOULD happen). First real-model run found asks answered with 'ok' were filed as plain promises and some asks were missed; the extraction prompt now says an ask stays an ask even when answered, with Hinglish examples (4 of 4 test requests became tasks after that). The model is not deterministic: one run dropped a 'client approved' feedback line another run caught. Not seen on the live group yet.

175. **Group summaries now include the real links -- 2026-10-21.** The summary model only ever sees `[link: site]` (links are swapped for the site name before it, and the fact-checker, read the chat), so links used to be missing. `group_digest` now adds the real URLs in code, never by the model: a point that cites a message with a link gets the URL(s) under it (max 3), and every other link shared in the window goes in a trailing *Links shared* list (sharer, time, URL, max 25). A message that also shares a password/OTP/key contributes no links. If the reply is too long the summary is trimmed, never the links list.

176. **Daily group summary DM at 22:30 -- 2026-10-21.** `group_digest.run_nightly()` (scheduler job `nightly_group_summary`, every 10 min on Railway, acts from 22:30 IST until midnight) DMs the checked group summary of the day (3 AM to now, links included, header 'Daily group summary') to Vidit and Kshitij (`app_settings nightly_group_summary_ids`, default `emp001,emp004`) via `wa_outbox`. Once a day per person (claim key `nightly_group_<date>_<id>`); a quiet day (no messages) sends nothing and stays claimed; a summary failure releases the claim so the next tick retries. Every day including weekends. Test: `POST /api/companion/nightly-group-summary?token=&to=emp003` (sends now to the named ids only). Not announced in What's new (leaders only). Verified on a temp DB with a fake model; not seen on a phone at 22:30 yet.

177. **Companion refused to start after a reboot because of a stale lock (pid reuse) -- fixed 2026-10-21.** After the always-on laptop was restarted, the 'Lumina companion' task exited at once with 'another companion instance is already running (pid 10576)'. `companion.lock` (`C:\lumina-logs\companion.lock`) still held a pid from days earlier, and Windows had given that number to an unrelated process (ShellHost.exe), so the old check (`_pid_alive`, just 'is that pid running') treated it as a live companion; with the task not running, no WhatsApp bridge, no outbox delivery, no reminders. Fix: `_acquire_singleton_lock` now also requires `_pid_is_companion(pid)` (the process command line must contain `laptop_agent`; if it can't be read it assumes yes, to stay safe about two instances forking the WhatsApp session). Recovery by hand if it ever recurs: read `C:\lumina-logs\agent.log` tail, delete `companion.lock` when its pid is not a laptop_agent, `Start-ScheduledTask -TaskName 'Lumina companion'`. A restart also leaves queued outbox messages pending, which flush in one burst when the bridge is back (up to 24 h old). Verified live: lock cleared, agent and bridge came up, `/health` connected, 41 queued messages delivered.

> [!NOTE]
> **WhatsApp daily-standup feature is NOT on `main`.** A design spec (`docs/superpowers/specs/2026-08-25-whatsapp-standup-design.md`) and implementation plan (`docs/superpowers/plans/2026-08-25-whatsapp-standup.md`) exist, and a branch `worktree-whatsapp-standup` carries a work-in-progress build (morning prompt, EOD nudge, `done`/`add`/`blocked` commands, founder digest + escalation). It is unverified and unmerged — don't treat it as shipped. Commit `e25f809` (gotcha #95) was deliberately cherry-picked *out* of that branch onto `main` so its fix could ship without the rest.170. **Reminders / scheduled group posts hardened, What's new history button -- 2026-10-19.** Live test of 25 phrasings against the real model showed it sometimes answered "Done" to "remind me ..." / "post in the group at 5pm" without calling a tool (nothing queued; chat history makes models imitate their own earlier "Done"). Fixes in `whatsapp_agent.py`: (1) `_SCHEDULE_Q` guard: such a request must run `remind_me` / `schedule_group_message` / `send_group_message` / `remind_teammate` / `message_multiple`; if not it is sent back once, then a clarifying question passes through or an honest "I couldn't set that up" replaces the fake "Done". (2) `group_digest` no longer hijacks "schedule a message for the group ..." (it returned "I'm not recording any group chat"). (3) A bare yes/ok with nothing pending (broadcast confirmations are consumed earlier) answers "Nothing is waiting for a yes" instead of letting the model invent a send (it claimed "Posted to the group" once); a yes after a stale confirmation prompt says it expired. (4) `remind_me` defaults a missing time (morning 09:00, evening 18:00, none 09:00) instead of asking. Harness: temp DB + real model, `D:/temp/remtest.py` style (set WHATSAPP_LLM=claude, `app_settings whatsapp_team_group`). **Dashboard morning message** (`morning_dash.py`) is also now standup-only (no Notion "On the board"), "Your decisions" only for emp001/emp004, and is saved into the person's chat memory so "what are 7 more" works; `_MORE_Q` fast path ("more", "show the rest", "what are N more") returns the full standup list. **What's new history:** `GET /api/whatsnew/all` (session) + `window.openWhatsNewHistory()` in `whatsnew.js`, opened by the "i" button in the dashboard nav; lists every published release, NEW tag on unseen ones. Lesson: in a Python heredoc a `\b` inside a normal string becomes a backspace char -- use raw strings or chr(92).

171. **"remind vidit at 3pm" answered with an old group-chat stub / sent at once -- fixed 2026-10-19.** (1) The stub "(I showed the group chat...)" saved after group answers was copied by the model as an answer to unrelated requests; group answers are now not saved to chat memory at all and any old stub is filtered out of loaded history. (2) `remind_teammate` had no time: "remind vidit at 3pm" was sent immediately. It now takes optional `when` (YYYY-MM-DD HH:MM IST, sent via outbox `send_after`; past times refused). (3) `_SCHEDULE_Q` guard also covers "remind <name> ..." and "tell <name> at/in/tomorrow ...". Tested with the real model on a temp DB (11 phrasings).

172. **Checkout reminder is now two-stage (supersedes #134's single 23:00 DM) -- 2026-10-19.** `job_checkout_nudge(cfg, second=False)` fires at `--checkout-nudge-time` (default **22:30**), `job_checkout_nudge_2` at `--checkout-nudge-time-2` (default **23:00**); both registered in `daily_jobs` (`checkout-nudge`, `checkout-nudge-2`, distinct names so the `_daily_fired` dedup works per stage). The list from `/api/companion/checkout-missing` (key `missing_checkout`) is recomputed live each time, so anyone who checked out after 22:30 is not messaged at 23:00. Max two DMs per person per day; Vidit/Kshitij still excluded server-side. Both texts carry the one-tap link `<url>/checkin.html?a=out` (browser geolocation, optional, denial still checks out; works from laptop or phone, laptop location is Wi-Fi-based and rough) plus "or reply out". Tested with a fake bridge (2 DMs per stage, correct text); not seen on a real phone. Scripts-only, reaches the always-on laptop via self-update.

178. **Group follow-ups hardened, slow-server message -- 2026-10-22.** A nudge to Vidit invented details for commitment #24 (quote was 'Can connect? @Vidit @Lakshmi', a request, not his promise). Now: `group_intel._too_vague` skips one-word actions with no due date and 'question + @mention' quotes; the extraction prompt says a bare call-out question is not a promise; `followups._compose` falls back to the plain text (which carries the real quote and time) if the model's text drops the quote; `POST /api/companion/group-commitments/reject?id=&note=` closes a wrong item. The bridge now says 'the server is slow right now...' on timeout/5xx instead of 'couldn't reach the assistant' (no auto-retry: a timed-out write may have gone through). Root cause of the failed reply was Railway being unresponsive about 11:08-11:14 IST (health timeouts also on 19/21/22 Oct); cause not found, no server logs here. Messages routed to Sonnet (advice, 'what should I do first', long) take 10-19 s by design.

179. **Railway outage 2026-10-22 to 10-26: pushes did not build.** Deploys were paused upstream (Railway incident); the live deployment stayed on the 2026-10-21 commit while commits c5a48b0..acb4aea (nudge fixes, WFH-only class-meeting link, meeting/Lumina link replies, no check-in before 06:00) waited. If the dashboard's newest deployment is older than the latest push, check status.railway.com and push a trivial commit to re-trigger.

180. **Sync All Tasks re-added old finished tasks to standups -- fixed 2026-10-28.** Notion keeps hundreds of old tasks as in_progress (655), and Sync All (auto_fill_standup, sync_all) pulls every in_progress task for every assignee, so on 2026-10-27 Lakshmi and Charulata got tasks due 23 Sep to 21 Oct back. Now (a) a task a person already marked done/deleted/delegated (any date) is never re-added for them (closed_pairs) and (b) Sync All skips tasks whose Notion due date is more than 14 days past (_stale_due). Personal Auto-Fill keeps old overdue tasks. Not changed: the tasks' Notion status, so they stay in_progress there.
